- **agentspace-runcomfy-wan-2-7**: Generate text-to-video with Wan 2.7 (Wan-AI's flagship motion model) on RunComfy. Calls `runcomfy run wan-ai/wan-2-7/text-to-video` through the local RunComfy CLI. Triggers on "wan", "wan 2.7", "wan-2-7", "wan video", or any explicit ask to generate video with this model. - **agentuity-database**: Use when adding relational data to an Agentuity app. Covers creating or linking managed Postgres, DATABASE_URL, pg, Drizzle ORM, pooling, transactions, migrations, safe parameter binding, and the narrow admin-script role for DBClient. ([agentuity/sdk](https://github.com/agentuity/sdk/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/agentuity-database) · ★19 · 2 copies)
- **agentuity-frameworks**: Use when adding Agentuity code to a framework app and choosing where routes, pages, server functions, config, or service clients belong. ([agentuity/sdk](https://github.com/agentuity/sdk/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/agentuity-frameworks) · ★19 · 2 copies)
- **agentuity-frontend**: When building website or app frontends that connect to Agentuity agents and services. ([agentuity/sdk](https://github.com/agentuity/sdk/tree/HEAD/packages/claude-code/skills/agentuity-frontend) · ★19)
- **agentuity-ops**: When running Agentuity CLI commands for deploying, managing databases, creating sandboxes, configuring storage, queues, cron jobs, email, or any cloud resource. - **agentuity-project**: Use when creating, importing, running, building, or deploying an Agentuity app. Covers framework-first project structure, agentuity.json, .env setup, agentuity dev, agentuity build, agentuity deploy, monorepo --dir usage, and deployment bundle inspection. ([agentuity/sdk](https://github.com/agentuity/sdk/tree/HEAD/packages/skills/skills/agentuity-project) · ★19 · 2 copies)
- **agentuity-project**: When deploying, hosting, or shipping websites, apps, or AI agents to production. - **ager-init-graph**: Scaffold an OKF Agent Graph (AGER) bundle with orchestrator/workers/judge layout, LoopPolicy, ScratchPad, FailurePolicy, and sample Tool. Use when starting multi-agent graphs, AGER configs, or loop-engineering scaffolds. Requires okf-plugin (okf-graph-eng). ([SpillwaveSolutions/okf-agent-graph](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/okf-agent-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/ager-init-graph) · ★1)
- **ager-pack**: Build a progressive-disclosure ContextPack from an AGER bundle. Default 1/4 window token budget, fail-closed. Bodies off unless that node is the pack root. ([SpillwaveSolutions/okf-agent-graph](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/okf-agent-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/ager-pack) · ★1)
- **ager-reverse-engineer**: End-to-end reverse engineer of agent codebases (Claude/ChatGPT APIs, LangChain/LangGraph, CrewAI, LlamaIndex, Claude Agent SDK, Deep Agents, MCP, AgentCore, microVMs) into a draft AGER OKF knowledge graph. Primary reverse entry skill for okf-agent-graph (AGKC). ([SpillwaveSolutions/okf-agent-graph](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/okf-agent-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/ager-reverse-engineer) · ★1)
- **ager-scan**: Scan a codebase for agent frameworks (LangGraph, CrewAI, LlamaIndex, Claude/OpenAI APIs, MCP, Deep Agents, hyperscalers, microVMs) and for Claude/Grok/Codex/universal plugin agents and skills. Extracts prompts, tools, schemas, orchestration, loops, harness signals, and plugin frontmatter. Use when reverse-engineering an existing agent project into AGER concepts. ([SpillwaveSolutions/okf-agent-graph](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/okf-agent-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/ager-scan) · ★1)
- **ager-session**: Open or close an isolated second-brain write session (worktree + PR). Use before writing knowledge when multiple agents share one knowledge remote. ([SpillwaveSolutions/okf-agent-graph](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/okf-agent-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/ager-session) · ★1)
- **ager-to-claude-agent-sdk**: Translate a validated AGER/OKF AgentGraph into a Claude Agent SDK lead + subagents project (max_turns, tools). ([SpillwaveSolutions/claude-agent-sdk-ager](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/claude-agent-sdk-ager/tree/HEAD/skills/ager-to-claude-agent-sdk))
- **ager-to-claude-code**: Translate a validated AGER/OKF AgentGraph into a Claude Code plugin (skills, agents, commands, hooks). Use when the user wants to compile an AGER graph for Claude Code. ([SpillwaveSolutions/claude-code-ager](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/claude-code-ager/tree/HEAD/skills/ager-to-claude-code))
- **ager-to-claude-managed-agents**: Translate a validated AGER/OKF AgentGraph into a Claude Managed Agents YAML manifest (entry, loop, agents). ([SpillwaveSolutions/claude-managed-agents-ager](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/claude-managed-agents-ager/tree/HEAD/skills/ager-to-claude-managed-agents))
- **ager-to-codex**: Translate a validated AGER/OKF AgentGraph into a Codex plugin ($ager-run skills). Use when compiling AGER for Codex. ([SpillwaveSolutions/codex-ager](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/codex-ager/tree/HEAD/skills/ager-to-codex))
- **ager-to-crewai**: Translate a validated AGER/OKF AgentGraph into a CrewAI hierarchical Crew (manager_agent, max_iter, tasks). ([SpillwaveSolutions/crewai-ager](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/crewai-ager/tree/HEAD/skills/ager-to-crewai))
- **ager-to-google-adk**: Translate a validated AGER/OKF AgentGraph into Google ADK (Python, TypeScript, Go, Java, Kotlin). Use for LlmAgent (non-graph) or Workflow graph (ADK 2.0). ([SpillwaveSolutions/google-adk-ager](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/google-adk-ager/tree/HEAD/skills/ager-to-google-adk))
- **ager-to-grok-build**: Translate a validated AGER/OKF AgentGraph into a Grok Build plugin (.grok-plugin, Claude-compatible skills, AGENTS.md). ([SpillwaveSolutions/grok-build-ager](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/grok-build-ager/tree/HEAD/skills/ager-to-grok-build))
- **ager-to-langchain-deep-agents**: Translate a validated AGER/OKF AgentGraph into a LangChain Deep Agents project (skills from AgentNodes, FilesystemBackend). ([SpillwaveSolutions/langchain-deep-agents-ager](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/langchain-deep-agents-ager/tree/HEAD/skills/ager-to-langchain-deep-agents))
- **ager-to-langgraph**: Translate a validated AGER/OKF AgentGraph into a LangGraph StateGraph (Send fan-out, LoopPolicy breaks, ScratchPad state). ([SpillwaveSolutions/langgraph-ager](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/langgraph-ager/tree/HEAD/skills/ager-to-langgraph))
- **ager-to-orca**: Translate a validated AGER/OKF AgentGraph into Orca ADE orchestration with named worktree agents, parallel fan-out, remote-control rename or disable, and official orca-cli + orchestration skills. ([SpillwaveSolutions/orca-ager](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/orca-ager/tree/HEAD/sample-orca/skills/ager-to-orca) · 2 copies)
- **ager-validate**: Validate AGER bundle structure and field rules, then delegate link/graph validation to okf-plugin (okf-graph-eng). Use before merge or after authoring multi-agent graphs. ([SpillwaveSolutions/okf-agent-graph](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/okf-agent-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/ager-validate) · ★1)
- **aget**: Browser CLI for LLM agents. Use when browser work, web page interaction, forms, auth, screenshots, scraping, QA, or dynamic-page inspection is needed. ALL browser work uses aget; never Playwright, Puppeteer, Selenium, Python/JS browser scripts, raw CDP, or an already-running browser. ([izzzzzi/agent-aget](https://github.com/izzzzzi/agent-aget/tree/HEAD/skills/aget) · ★11)
- **aget**: Use the local aget CLI for agent-friendly URL fetching, local markdown extraction, and explicit user-authorized session replay. Use when a task needs page content through `aget`, authenticated/gated-page fetches, OAuth/session import from the user's real browser, session composition, cmux or Chrome session import, or safe local-only web context workflows.
